<span class="ct-node-title-full">Why the Cash Landrum Case Still Defies Resolution</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">The Cash–Landrum UFO incident was a reported close encounter on 29 December 1980 near Huffman, Texas, involving Betty Cash, Vickie Landrum and Landrum’s seven-year-old grandson, Colby. They said a brilliant, diamond-shaped object hovered over a rural road, emitted intense heat and flame, and was later accompanied by numerous military-style helicopters.</span>
</span>
